particular parser syntax for nerds:

PROMOTION (DAY) MM/DD/YYYY ArenaName [linkURL linkName]
MatchNo) side1-1, side1-2, side1-3 DECISION side2-1, side2-2, side2-3 [matchstip]

special codes
! at start of event header means multiple events at same venue on same day.
(c) champion obviously
## note attached to preceding match
&& note attached to event
%% attendance (always integer, always suspect)
** event name
**********************/

AAA TV (FRI) 01/12/1996 Arena Neza [RB, http://youtu.be/DgTH6CN1t1o RobViper (Music Video), SuperLuchas 251, SuperLuchas 252, SuperLuchas 254, WON 1996-1-12]
Attendance: sellout
1) La Rosa, La Sirenita DQ Martha Villalobos, Neftaly
## straight falls
2) Kid Guzman, Salsero b Pimpinela Escarlata, Súper Crazy
3) Boomerang, Discovery, Ludxor, Thunderbird, Venum I DQ Bronco (UWA), El Duende, Marabunta, Mr. Cóndor, Rocky Santana
## this is the start of the New Diabolicos vs Old Diabolicos
4) El Mexicano, Volador COR Fuerza Guerrera, Juventud Guerrera [MEX TAG]
## Back on 12/13/1995, PROMELL did a change of the MEX TRIOS titles by counting out Psicosis (when he was in Japan), declaring the titles vacant, and then having Panther, Fuerza and Signo win as the new team. This is AAA answering back a month later. Fuerza Guerrera is not here, and they do the same bit of the commission counting him out to declare the titles vacant before going over to the real match. Arturo Rivera mentions this match (saying the commission ordered Fuerza to defend even though he's in another company) in the opening of the AAA show, but TV instead skips to the start of the real title match.
5) Juventud Guerrera, Psicosis b El Mexicano, Volador I [MEX TAG]
## Psicosis replaced Fuerza Guerrera. Juvy and Psicosis get the actual tag team title belts. The commission later undoes all of this and Fuerza and Juventud end up as champs again. (The exact same trios change appears not to be undone.) Listed as the IWAS tag titles, but TV bills it as the national titles, and those are the ones shown.
6) La Parka, Máscara Sagrada, Octagón DQ Killer, Pentagon, Pierroth
## Before the main event there was a ceremony to honor Blue Demon which featured Blue Demon Jr's AAA debut & an angle where Pierroth Jr attacked both
&& An Espectrito I & Espectrito II vs Baby Rabbit & Súper Muñequito match has been listed here, but isn't in the lineup or the results.

&& 1st show using AAA talent, card drastically changes to reflect CMLL pulling anyone they controlled. More useful as a sign of who CMLL is employing here than anything. Juan Herrera has taken over booking here, with the idea that they're going to be neutral and allow people from CMLL or AAA to work here. CMLL pulled wrestlers from a 01/18 Herrera run show over booking Mascara Sagrada and Super Muneco being booked

&& SuperLuchas calls this show a complete failure. PROMELL, speaking to Box y Lucha 2249, said attendance wasn't as bad as claimed, and it wasn't even technically a PROMELL show; it was a Cronistas Deportivas show where PROMELL supplied the talent. It still airs as the first PROMELL TV show, though CMLL wrestlers appearing on Televisa are banned from appearing. PROMELL staff member Jose Luis Pina was said to be assualted at the show.

&& SuperLuchas 271 has the origina lineup for this show: Perro Aguayo/Konnan/Pierroth Jr. vs La Parka/Cien Caras/Killer [Relevos Incriebles] + Rey Misterio Jr. vs Juventud Guerrera [Car vs Car] + Octagon/Ultimo Dragon/Hong Kong Lee vs Villano III, Villano IV, Villano V [Lumberjack] + Octagon/Ultimo Dragon/Hong Kong Lee (debut) vs Villano III, Villano IV, Villano V [Lumberjack] + Torero/Salsero/El Mexicano vs Tony Arce/Rocco Valente/Vulcano. SuperLuchas 273 updates it to the historically listed lineup but Ultimo Dragon being in the Campeon de Campones match. WON reports 7,000 attendance. SuperLuchas 274 has many columns excusing a bad turnout (weather, Chavez fight, start time confusion), which suggests it drew much less. SuperLuchas also mentions many incidents between fans and wrestlrs.

&& SuperLuchas 273 previews this card as on 06/29 in El Toreo with a bunch of matches that don't happen: Mascara Sagrada/Pantera/Ultimo Dragon vs Villano III, Villano IV, Villano V [Lumberjack] + Salsero/Torero/El Mexicano vs Tony Arce/Rocco Valente/Vulcano + Mascarita Sagrada Jr./Super Munequito/Torerito vs Espectrito I/Espectrito II/Mini Yeti. SuperLuchas 276 has Arturto Rivera inviting the many enemies of AAA (CMLL and PROMELL heads) to come to this show to understand how wrestling is supposed to look. SuperLuchas 277 says the MEX ATOMICOS is scheduled to start on this show; it does not. This airs over two weeks, which means the 07/12 taping in Zacatecas never aired.
